'{"tools":[{"name":"mcp__21st__get_usage","description":"Check the caller\'s component retrieval tier/quota and aiGenerationEnabled. Paid component access does not imply hosted AI access. When aiGenerationEnabled is false, use search and get_component, then adapt the code with your own coding agent; do not call generate or iterate_generation. This reports AI entitlement and the remaining AI credit balance when available. This tool is part of plugin `21st.dev`.\\n\\nexec tool declaration:\\n```ts\\ndeclare const tools: { mcp__21st__get_usage(args: { [key: string]: unknown; }): Promise<CallToolResult<{\\n  // Whether this account has hosted AI access. This is an entitlement, not a credit balance. When false, use search and get_component, then adapt the code with your own agent.\\n  aiGenerationEnabled?: boolean;\\n  // Daily free component-code retrieval allowance; null for paid callers.\\n  freeRetrievalsPerDay?: number | null;\\n  // Free retrievals left today; null for paid callers.\\n  freeRetrievalsRemaining?: number | null;\\n  // Always null today (search is unmetered).\\n  freeSearchesPerDay?: number | null;\\n  // Always null today (search is unmetered).\\n  freeSearchesRemaining?: number | null;\\n  // free | paid.\\n  tier?: string;\\n  // Upgrade link; null for paid callers.\\n  upgradeUrl?: string | null;\\n  [key: string]: unknown;\\n}>>; };\\n```"},{"name":"mcp__21st__get_component","description":"Retrieve the full code for one component result: the component source AND its demo (usage example) together, plus the install command and registry dependencies. Pass the `id` (a DEMO id) from any search / list_bookmarks / get_bookmark_list / list_team_components result. PAID: on the free tier this consumes one of your daily retrievals and may instead return a paywall (structuredContent.locked=true) or not-found (found=false) rather than code - check before treating the text as source. A successful result also carries structuredContent.quota with the retrievals you have left today. For a 21st Video block (search with runtime \'remotion\') the result also carries structuredContent.component.video: the /r/video install, a <Composition> snippet for src/Root.tsx, the Remotion license line and an editor link. This tool is part of plugin `21st.dev`.\\n\\nexec tool declaration:\\n```ts\\ndeclare const tools: { mcp__21st__get_component(args: {\\n  // The `id` (a demo id) from a search/list result.\\n  id: number;\\n}): Promise<CallToolResult<{\\n  // The retrieved component bundle (success case only).\\n  component?: {\\n  // Component author\'s user id, null when unknown.\\n  authorUserId?: string | null;\\n  // The component\'s full TSX source.\\n  componentCode?: string;\\n  // The parent component\'s id.\\n  componentId?: number;\\n  // The demo\'s TSX source (empty string when its fetch failed).\\n  demoCode?: string;\\n  // Demo (usage example) name.\\n  demoName?: string;\\n  // Description, null when absent.\\n  description?: string | null;\\n  // The demo id that was retrieved.\\n  id?: number;\\n  // shadcn install command (empty string when it cannot be built).\\n  installCommand?: string;\\n  // False for a private (team/personal-registry) component.\\n  isPublic?: boolean;\\n  // Component name.\\n  name?: string;\\n  // Preview image URL, null when absent.\\n  previewUrl?: string | null;\\n  // Resolved registry dependency tree; loosely-typed payload from resolveRegistryDependencyTree, absent when there are none.\\n  registryDependencies?: unknown;\\n  // Owning team\'s id for a team-registry component, else null.\\n  registryTeamId?: string | null;\\n  // Owning user\'s id for a personal-registry component, else null.\\n  registryUserId?: string | null;\\n  // 21st Video blocks only (absent for UI components): install, mount and license facts.\\n  video?: {\\n  // Supported aspects (16:9 | 9:16 | 1:1).\\n  aspects?: Array<string>;\\n  // A <Composition> for src/Root.tsx: fps and default duration from the block meta, size from its first aspect.\\n  compositionSnippet?: string;\\n  // Opens the 21st video editor with this block.\\n  editorUrl?: string;\\n  // Video block kind, null when unknown.\\n  kind?: string | null;\\n  // The Built with Remotion license line to keep with the code.\\n  license?: string;\\n  // The gated /r/video/<author>/<slug> registry item URL (append ?api_key=[REDACTED]  registryItem?: string;\\n  // Always \\"remotion\\".\\n  runtime?: string;\\n  [key: string]: unknown;\\n};\\n  [key: string]: unknown;\\n};\\n  // Present and false only when no component exists (or is accessible) for the id; absent on success and on paywall.\\n  found?: boolean;\\n  // The daily free limit that was hit (paywall shape; may be absent).\\n  limit?: number;\\n  // True on the paywall denial shape (free retrieval quota exhausted).\\n  locked?: boolean;\\n  // What the caller has left after this retrieval; only attached for metered (public, free-tier-relevant) retrievals.\\n  quota?: {\\n  // Daily free retrieval allowance; null for paid callers.\\n  perDay?: number | null;\\n  // Retrievals left today; null for paid callers.\\n  remaining?: number | null;\\n  // When the daily bucket resets; null when not applicable.\\n  resetAt?: string | null;\\n  // free | paid.\\n  tier?: string;\\n  // Upgrade link; null for paid callers.\\n  upgradeUrl?: string | null;\\n  [key: string]: unknown;\\n};\\n  // Paywall denial reason, e.g. retrieval_limit_reached (paywall shape only).\\n  reason?: string;\\n  // When the free quota resets (paywall shape; may be absent or null).\\n  resetAt?: string | null;\\n  // Link to upgrade/checkout (paywall shape; may be absent).\\n  upgradeUrl?: string;\\n  [key: string]: unknown;\\n}>>; };\\n```"}]}'}]

This launch marked its transition from an R&D hub to a commercial leader.\\",\\n  },\\n  {\\n    date: \\"2023\\",\\n    title: \\"Global Expansion\\",\\n    content:\\n      \\"Ruixen expanded to multiple countries, partnering with leading enterprises and tech firms. Its solutions became known for reliability, scalability, and adaptability.\\",\\n  },\\n  {\\n    date: \\"2025\\",\\n    title: \\"Ruixen AI Cloud\\",\\n    content:\\n      \\"The company launched Ruixen AI Cloud, offering a unified ecosystem for AI training, deployment, and monitoring, setting new industry benchmarks for performance.\\",\\n  },\\n];\\n\\nexport default function Timeline_02() {\\n  return (\\n    <section className=\\"bg-background py-24\\">\\n      <div className=\\"container\\">\\n        <h1 className=\\"text-foreground mb-16 text-center text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-5xl\\">\\n          The Journey of Ruixen\\n        </h1>\\n\\n        <div className=\\"relative mx-auto max-w-3xl\\">\\n          {/* Subtle vertical line */}\\n\\n          {timelineData.map((entry, index) => (\\n            <motion.div\\n              key={index}\\n              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}\\n              whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}\\n dot */}\\n              <div className=\\"absolute left-2 top-5 h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-cyan-500 ring-4 ring-background\\" />\\n\\n              {/* Content */}\\n              <h4 className=\\"text-lg font-normal text-foreground\\">\\n                {entry.title}\\n              </h4>\\n              <p className=\\"mb-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">{entry.date}</p>\\n text-muted-foreground\\">\\n                    {entry.content}\\n                  </p>\\n                </CardContent>\\n              </Card>\\n            </motion.div>\\n          ))}\\n        </div>\\n      </div>\\n    </section>\\n  );\\n}\\n```\\n\\n## Demo (usage)\\n```tsx\\nimport Timeline_02 from \\"@/components/ui/timeline-02\\";\\n\\nexport default function DemoOne() {\\n  return <Timeline_02 />;\\n}\\n\\n```\\n\\n1 of 2 free component retrievals left today.
